# Qualys Security Inspector

Portable contract for the shipped Qualys scan coverage, asset inventory, vulnerability management, and administration assessments over the Qualys VM/PC, Administration, Asset Management, Cloud Agent, and WAS APIs.

## Purpose

Qualys Security Inspector gives auditors a read-only view of a Qualys subscription: whether vulnerability scans are scheduled, authenticated, current, and reach every network segment, whether asset groups, scanner appliances, Cloud Agents, cloud connectors, and tags keep the asset inventory complete, whether open detections are remediated within their SLA and patchable ones are tracked, and who can administer the subscription, how reports are distributed, whether web applications are scanned, and whether activity is logged.

## Rationale

Qualys shows each user only what its role and scope tags allow. A Manager or Super User sees the whole subscription, while other roles see only the assets and objects in their scope. A portable implementation must keep that difference visible instead of reading a scoped view as a compliant subscription, so when the configured user is verified to hold another role or to carry scope tags, every check that would otherwise pass is capped at warn.

Most lists arrive through WARNING/URL continuations or QPS searches that stop at a record or page cap, and some answers are a single document whose continuation is never followed. Each of those conditions marks the list partial and caps the checks built on it, so a list that was only partly read cannot pass. Several controls, such as asset group completeness, report distribution, and option profile review, need facts the API does not expose, so they never pass on API evidence alone.

## Runtime behavior

- The tool only reads. QPS searches are POST requests that change nothing, and the OAuth mode's token request to the platform gateway is the only other POST.
- Lists stop at 5000 records and 25 pages unless the list states another cap; hosts and detections stop at host_limit and detection_limit.
- A refused or failed read never counts as an empty list: the checks that need it are manual and name the read, and when Qualys answers that a module is unavailable the finding says the module may be unlicensed or the role not permitted.
- A throttled or failed read is retried before it counts as failed: HTTP 409, 429, and 5xx answers, timeouts, and network errors get up to QUALYS_MAX_RETRIES more attempts, each waiting as long as X-RateLimit-ToWait-Sec or Retry-After asks, up to 30 seconds. A read that still fails names its endpoint, the status or the timeout, and the number of attempts.
- The knowledge base is looked up only for the QIDs of open detections, 200 QIDs per request and at most 2000 QIDs.
- The WAS scan history search runs only for web applications the lookback scan search did not resolve, and is recorded as not collected when no web application needed it or when an earlier WAS read failed.
- Agent activation keys are read only to tell whether one is present and are never exported.

## Non-goals

- Changing Qualys settings, launching scans, or issuing any administrative write request
- Reconciling asset groups against an authoritative inventory such as a CMDB or IPAM
- Confirming report recipients, log retention, or review cadence, which the API does not expose
- Evaluating CloudView resources or Policy Compliance posture detail
- Treating a scoped view as complete subscription coverage
- Reproducing a particular programming language, package layout, or command-line framework

## Portable implementation guidance

Keep the API client, continuation handling, evidence projection, verdict evaluation, and bundle writer as separable concerns. Count from the full collected arrays at the decision point rather than from evidence previews. Keep each dataset's read state (read, refused, not collected because an earlier read failed, or partial) next to its records so every finding can say which sources it depended on and why a pass was capped.

## Shared integration contract

This specification requires [shared integration contract version 1.1](./integration-contract.md). The raw contract is available at https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ethanolivertroy/grclanker/main/specs/integration-contract.md.

## Known runtime gaps

- A WARNING/URL continuation that repeats the previous page's URL is requested again until the 25-page cap, so the list can collect duplicate records.
- Authentication records are summarized as a count per technology, so record status and expiry are never evaluated.

### Parameters

#### `qualys_check_access`

| Parameter | Kind | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `username` | string | no | Qualys API username. Defaults to QUALYS_USERNAME or the config file. |
| `password` | string | no | Qualys API password. Defaults to QUALYS_PASSWORD or the config file. |
| `token` | string | no | Pre-issued bearer token used instead of basic auth. Defaults to QUALYS_TOKEN. |
| `platform` | string | no | Qualys platform ID (US1, US2, US3, US4, GOV1, EU1, EU2, EU3, IN1, CA1, AE1, UK1, AU1, KSA1), API server hostname, or https URL. Defaults to QUALYS_PLATFORM or US1. |
| `base_url` | string | no | Explicit API server base URL overriding the platform mapping. Defaults to QUALYS_BASE_URL. |
| `gateway_url` | string | no | API Gateway URL used only for OAuth token requests. Defaults to the platform gateway. |
| `use_oauth` | boolean | no | Request a JWT from the platform gateway /auth endpoint with the username and password. Defaults to QUALYS_USE_OAUTH. |
| `config_file` | string | no | Path to a key=value or INI config file with username, password, platform. Defaults to QUALYS_CONFIG_FILE or ~/.qcrc. |
| `timeout_seconds` | number | no | HTTP timeout in seconds. Defaults to 60. |
| `lookback_days` | number | no | Lookback window in days for scans, reports, activity, and WAS scans. Defaults to 30. |

## Authentication

Supported modes:

- Basic authentication: Authorization: Basic with the base64 encoding of username:password
- Pre-issued bearer token: Authorization: Bearer with the configured token
- Gateway token exchange: the username and password are exchanged at the platform gateway for a bearer token

Credential precedence, highest first:

1. Explicit tool arguments
2. QUALYS_* environment variables
3. Qualys key=value config file
4. A token from any source selects bearer authentication; otherwise use_oauth selects the gateway token exchange; otherwise basic authentication

Environment variables: `QUALYS_CONFIG_FILE`, `QUALYS_USERNAME`, `QUALYS_USER`, `QUALYS_PASSWORD`, `QUALYS_TOKEN`, `QUALYS_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `QUALYS_USE_OAUTH`, `QUALYS_PLATFORM`, `QUALYS_API_SERVER`, `QUALYS_BASE_URL`, `QUALYS_API_URL`, `QUALYS_GATEWAY_URL`, `QUALYS_TIMEOUT`, `QUALYS_MAX_RETRIES`, `QUALYS_LOOKBACK_DAYS`

Configuration locations: ~/.qcrc, Explicit path from config_file or QUALYS_CONFIG_FILE

Credential and deployment variants: Named platforms US1 (the default), US2, US3, US4, GOV1, EU1, EU2, EU3, IN1, CA1, AE1, UK1, AU1, and KSA1, each with its own API server and gateway, An API server hostname or https URL that belongs to a named platform resolves to that platform, Any other API server host is a custom platform whose gateway replaces a leading qualysapi. with qualysgateway., unless gateway_url overrides it

Configuration fields: `username`, `user`, `password`, `token`, `use_oauth`, `platform`, `hostname`, `base_url`, `gateway_url`, `timeout`

Malformed configuration: Reject malformed or ambiguous configuration before any request; never echo credential values.

Credential refresh: POST {gateway}/auth with the form fields username, password, and token=true when use_oauth is set and no token is configured; the returned token is reused for 3.5 hours.

### Request construction

| Surface | Input | Exact value or rule | Required |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scheduled_scans` | client | Use the configured API server (base_url, else the selected platform's API server); never follow a continuation URL to another origin. | yes |
| `scheduled_scans` | headers | X-Requested-With: grclanker; Accept: application/xml; Authorization: {Basic base64(username:password), or Bearer token} | yes |
| `scheduled_scans` | response | XML in which every <SCAN> element is one record and an optional WARNING carries a continuation URL; a SIMPLE_RETURN, GENERIC_RETURN, or root-level ERROR envelope, a non-XML body, or HTTP status 400 or above is a refusal. | yes |
| `scheduled_scans` | query:action | list | yes |
| `scheduled_scans` | query:show_notifications | 0 | yes |
| `scheduled_scans` | query:id_min | The id_min carried by the previous page's WARNING/URL; Only on a continuation, which requests the WARNING/URL exactly as returned | no |
| `scans` | client | Use the configured API server (base_url, else the selected platform's API server); never follow a continuation URL to another origin. | yes |
| `scans` | headers | X-Requested-With: grclanker; Accept: application/xml; Authorization: {Basic base64(username:password), or Bearer token} | yes |
| `scans` | response | XML in which every <SCAN> element is one record and an optional WARNING carries a continuation URL; a SIMPLE_RETURN, GENERIC_RETURN, or root-level ERROR envelope, a non-XML body, or HTTP status 400 or above is a refusal. | yes |
| `scans` | query:action | list | yes |
| `scans` | query:launched_after_datetime | lookback_days before the assessment time, in UTC ISO 8601 without fractional seconds | yes |
| `scans` | query:show_ags | 1 | yes |
| `scans` | query:show_op | 1 | yes |
| `scans` | query:id_min | The id_min carried by the previous page's WARNING/URL; Only on a continuation, which requests the WARNING/URL exactly as returned | no |

## Pagination

| Surfaces | Cursor or marker | Page size | Item cap | Page cap | Total semantics | Stop conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `scheduled_scans`, `scans`, `option_profiles`, `appliances`, `compliance_policies`, `scheduled_reports`, `reports` | `WARNING/URL` | service default | 5000 | 25 | Each page holds as many records as Qualys returns. Qualys reports no total: a page without a WARNING/URL continuation ends the list, and a continuation left at the item cap or at the 25-page cap marks it partial. | A page without a WARNING/URL continuation; 5000 records collected; The 25-page cap; An empty page that still carries a continuation, which marks the list partial; A continuation URL on another origin, which is not followed and marks the list partial |
| `hosts` | `WARNING/URL` | service default | caller limit | 25 | Pages of the smaller of host_limit and 1000 hosts (truncation_limit), up to host_limit hosts (default 5000, set from 1 through 200000). Qualys reports no total: a page without a WARNING/URL continuation ends the list, and a continuation left at the item cap or at the 25-page cap marks it partial. | A page without a WARNING/URL continuation; host_limit hosts collected; The 25-page cap; An empty page that still carries a continuation, which marks the list partial; A continuation URL on another origin, which is not followed and marks the list partial |
| `asset_groups` | `WARNING/URL` | 500 | 5000 | 25 | Pages of 500 asset groups (truncation_limit). Qualys reports no total: a page without a WARNING/URL continuation ends the list, and a continuation left at the item cap or at the 25-page cap marks it partial. | A page without a WARNING/URL continuation; 5000 asset groups collected; The 25-page cap; An empty page that still carries a continuation, which marks the list partial; A continuation URL on another origin, which is not followed and marks the list partial |
| `detections` | `WARNING/URL` | service default | caller limit | 25 | Pages of the smaller of detection_limit and 1000 hosts (truncation_limit), flattened to one record per detection, up to detection_limit detections (default 5000, set from 1 through 200000). Qualys reports no total: a page without a WARNING/URL continuation ends the list, and a continuation left at the item cap or at the 25-page cap marks it partial. | A page without a WARNING/URL continuation; detection_limit detections collected; The 25-page cap; An empty page that still carries a continuation, which marks the list partial; A continuation URL on another origin, which is not followed and marks the list partial |
| `knowledge_base` | `WARNING/URL`, `ids` | service default | 5000 | 25 | The distinct QIDs of open detections are requested in batches of 200 (ids), at most 2000 QIDs; QIDs past 2000 are not requested and mark the lookup partial. Qualys reports no total: a page without a WARNING/URL continuation ends a batch, and a continuation left at 5000 records or at the 25-page cap marks the lookup partial. | A page without a WARNING/URL continuation; 5000 records collected in a batch; The 25-page cap; An empty page that still carries a continuation, which marks the list partial; A continuation URL on another origin, which is not followed and marks the list partial; Every batch of up to 200 QIDs requested |
| `excluded_ips`, `auth_records` | `WARNING/URL` | service default | none | 1 | One read; a WARNING/URL continuation is not followed and marks the list partial. | The first response |
| `user_list` | None | service default | none | 1 | One read of the whole user list, which carries no continuation. | The first response |
| `activity_log` | None | 5000 | 5000 | 1 | One read of up to 5000 rows since the lookback start (truncation_limit); 5000 rows mark the log partial, because older rows in the window may be left. | The first response |
| `users` | `hasMoreRecords`, `lastId` | service default | 5000 | 25 | Pages of 500 users (limitResults); each continuation repeats the search with the criterion id GREATER than the previous page's lastId. hasMoreRecords, or a full page when it is absent, means more records follow; more records left at 5000 users or at the 25-page cap mark the list partial. | hasMoreRecords false, or a page shorter than limitResults when hasMoreRecords is absent; A page without lastId, which marks the list partial when more records follow; An empty page; 5000 users collected; The 25-page cap |
| `connectors`, `was_webapps`, `was_scans`, `was_auth_records`, `was_schedules` | `hasMoreRecords`, `lastId` | service default | 5000 | 25 | Pages of 100 records (limitResults); each continuation repeats the search with the criterion id GREATER than the previous page's lastId. hasMoreRecords, or a full page when it is absent, means more records follow; more records left at 5000 records or at the 25-page cap mark the list partial. | hasMoreRecords false, or a page shorter than limitResults when hasMoreRecords is absent; A page without lastId, which marks the list partial when more records follow; An empty page; 5000 records collected; The 25-page cap |
| `was_scan_history` | `webApp.id`, `hasMoreRecords`, `lastId` | service default | 5000 | 25 | The web applications without a finished vulnerability scan in the lookback search are requested in batches of 100 IDs (webApp.id IN), each walked in pages of 100 scans (limitResults) with the criterion id GREATER than the previous page's lastId. hasMoreRecords, or a full page when it is absent, means more records follow; a batch left with more records at the 25-page cap or on a page without lastId stops the history, and web applications left unrequested at 5000 scans mark it partial. | Every batch of up to 100 web application IDs requested; A batch that stops with more records left, which marks the history partial; 5000 scans collected across batches, which marks the history partial when web applications are left unrequested |
| `cloud_agents` | `hasMoreRecords`, `lastId` | service default | caller limit | 25 | Pages of 1000 agents (limitResults), up to host_limit agents (default 5000, set from 1 through 200000); each continuation repeats the search with the criterion id GREATER than the previous page's lastId. hasMoreRecords, or a full page when it is absent, means more records follow; more records left at host_limit agents or at the 25-page cap mark the list partial. | hasMoreRecords false, or a page shorter than limitResults when hasMoreRecords is absent; A page without lastId, which marks the list partial when more records follow; An empty page; host_limit agents collected; The 25-page cap |
| `tags` | `hasMoreRecords`, `lastId` | service default | 2000 | 25 | Pages of 1000 tags (limitResults); each continuation repeats the search with the criterion id GREATER than the previous page's lastId. hasMoreRecords, or a full page when it is absent, means more records follow; more records left at 2000 tags or at the 25-page cap mark the list partial. | hasMoreRecords false, or a page shorter than limitResults when hasMoreRecords is absent; A page without lastId, which marks the list partial when more records follow; An empty page; 2000 tags collected; The 25-page cap |

## Rate limits

| Scope | Documented limit | Retry headers | Retryable statuses | Policy |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Qualys Security Inspector | Subscription-specific: responses report X-RateLimit-Limit calls per X-RateLimit-Window-Sec seconds and X-Concurrency-Limit-Limit concurrent calls. | `X-RateLimit-ToWait-Sec`, `Retry-After` | 409, 429, 500-599 | Retry 409, 429, every 5xx status, requests that time out (60 seconds by default), and network errors up to QUALYS_MAX_RETRIES times after the first attempt (3 by default). Every Qualys request only reads, the QPS search POSTs and the gateway token request included, so each one is retried. Wait X-RateLimit-ToWait-Sec seconds when a retried response carries it as a number, otherwise Retry-After as delta-seconds or as an HTTP date measured against the client clock, otherwise 2, 4, then 8 seconds, doubling for any further retry; every wait is capped at 30 seconds. A failed gateway token request is retried by its own retries only, never again around the read that needed the token. When the retries run out the read fails with the endpoint path, the HTTP status or the timeout, and the number of attempts. |

### Ordered decision rules

Rules are evaluated from lowest order number to highest. The first matching condition determines the finding status; later rules are not evaluated.

A `matches` condition performs a regular-expression search; anchors are required for whole-value matching, and an `i` flag requests case-insensitive matching. A `ratio` condition divides the numerator by the denominator, applies the declared scale, and rounds to the declared decimal places by choosing the nearest value with exact half cases rounded toward positive infinity; a zero, null, or missing denominator does not match.

| Finding | Order | Outcome | First-match condition | Explanatory note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `QUALYS-C01` | 1 | manual | `qualys_c01_branch_01_matches` equals true |  |
| `QUALYS-C01` | 2 | fail | `qualys_c01_branch_02_matches` equals true |  |
| `QUALYS-C01` | 3 | manual | `qualys_c01_branch_03_matches` equals true |  |
| `QUALYS-C01` | 4 | manual | `qualys_c01_branch_04_matches` equals true |  |
| `QUALYS-C01` | 5 | warn | `qualys_c01_branch_05_matches` equals true |  |
| `QUALYS-C01` | 6 | warn | `qualys_c01_branch_06_matches` equals true |  |
| `QUALYS-C01` | 7 | pass | `qualys_c01_branch_07_matches` equals true |  |
| `QUALYS-C02` | 1 | manual | `qualys_c02_branch_01_matches` equals true |  |

## Collection states

| State | Required rendering |
|---|---|
| complete | complete: proven API exhaustion or a successful single-object read. |
| truncated | truncated: preserve seen and total when available plus the exact stop reason. |
| unreadable | unreadable: render data and counts as null and retain a scrubbed error envelope. |
| denied | denied: render null evidence with the endpoint and HTTP status, never an empty inventory. |
| not requested | not_requested: identify the unreadable parent dependency and do not invent an HTTP status. |
| not configured | not_configured: identify the absent optional feature or credential without treating it as compliant. |

## Integration-specific scrubbing

Shared contract version: 1.1.

Projection stage: Every surface keeps only its declared fields, the Qualys raw data allowlist, before evidence export, and every bundle file is scrubbed of the configured credential values.

Sensitive fields and values: password, token, Authorization, activationKey

Credential formats: Qualys username and password, sent as Basic authentication or exchanged at the platform gateway, Qualys bearer token

Reviewed benign exceptions: Stable non-secret resource identifiers and public documentation URLs remain visible unless carried in a credential field.

Integration-specific rules:

- Withhold undocumented error bodies; retain only status, media type, byte length, and allowlisted vendor error codes.
- Remove URL user information, queries, and fragments from evidence and reject off-origin pagination links.
- Unavailable counts, arrays, maps, and negative flags are null rather than fabricated empty values.

| `core_data/access.json` | json | Always. | The projected runtime dataset or its explicit unavailable marker. | UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline. |

### Record schemas

#### finding

- `id`
- `title`
- `severity`
- `status`
- `summary`
- `evidence`
- `framework mappings`

#### collection_marker

- `collected`
- `status`
- `endpoint`
- `error`

#### bundle_result

- `outputDir`
- `zipPath`
- `fileCount`
- `findingCount`
- `errorCount`

#### assessment

- `title or category`
- `summary`
- `findings`
- `errors when collection was partial`

#### pagination_state

- `items or rows seen`
- `reported total when available`
- `pages`
- `truncated`
- `stop reason`

JSON formatting: UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline.

Overwrite policy: Allocate qualys-{platform}-audit-bundle, or the first of -2 through -99 whose directory and zip are both free, and fail rather than overwrite.

Path safety: Resolve beneath the configured output root and reject traversal, unsafe parents, files, and symbolic-link escapes.

Archive pairing: Write a sibling zip named from the exact allocated bundle-directory path plus .zip.
